## 2. THE ALGORITHMIC RECONSTRUCTION RULES (ANTIDOTE TO SPAM FLAGS)
In late 2025 and 2026, YouTube heavily intensified its **"Inauthentic Content"** (previously "Repetitious Content") detection system. Programmatic templates are now heavily suppressed. To bypass these automated filters and regain Browse, Shorts Feed, and Recommendation reach, **every video optimization must strictly adhere to the following rules:**

### Title Optimization Rules:
* **NO brackets** at the beginning of the title (e.g., do not use `[📺]` or `[⚡]`).
* **NO rigid programmatic structures** (e.g., do not use `BAND - SONG | Rocksmith Cover`).
* **DO** write highly conversational, engaging, "human-crafted" titles. Highlight the vibe of the song, the difficulty of the bassline, or a personal reaction (e.g., *"This Huey Lewis bassline is a serious forearm workout!"*).
* **VARY your starting words!** Do not start multiple consecutive Shorts titles with identical phrases (such as starting multiple titles with "Laying down..."). Use a variety of action verbs and structures (e.g. "Diving into...", "Plucking out...", "Bringing back...", "Grooving on...") to keep the automatic similarity detection engines clean!

### Description Optimization Rules:
* **NO copy-pasting massive link clouds** at the top of descriptions.
* **The first 2-3 lines must be 100% unique** to that specific video. Describe the play-along, the artist, or how the bassline felt to play.
* **Limit outbound links** to a maximum of 2 or 3 essential locations (typically Kick, Twitch, and Discord). Too many outbound links trigger YouTube's "link spam" algorithms.
* Use a clean, compact "Gear & Bio" section in the lower half of the description, but keep it brief and customized.

### Pinned Comment Rules:
* **NO copy-pasting links** in pinned comments. Mass-pinning link templates is flagged as comment spamming.
* **DO** write engaging, conversational questions to spark user engagement (e.g., *"What is your favorite bass track by this artist? Let me know!"*).

### Tag Box Rules:
* **NO copy-pasting a list of 20+ generic tags** across all videos.
* **DO** use only **3 to 5 highly specific tags** relevant strictly to the specific song and artist (e.g., `huey lewis and the news`, `power of love bass cover`, `rocksmith bass`, `mavo music`).

### Visual/Shorts Rules:
* Focus on creating high-retention visual hooks (especially a 3-second face/voice intro) to break the "silent gameplay" penalty under the Inauthentic Content guidelines.

### 🚨 ZERO REPETITION / ABSOLUTE UNIQUENESS RULE (added 2026-07-14 — MANDATORY)
**This rule overrides everything else. YouTube's Inauthentic Content detector now flags *semantic* repetition (same opening words, same sentence skeleton, same bio template, same pinned-comment phrasing) even when the song/artist names are swapped out. To stay clear of the filter:**
* **EVERY title (long-form AND Shorts) must be 100% unique**, even for re-uploads of the same song. No two titles may share the same opening word, opening phrase, or sentence structure.
* **EVERY description must be 100% unique**:
  - The first 2–3 lines (hook) must be hand-written commentary specific to that playthrough.
  - The bio/CTA block must be rewritten fresh every time — do NOT rotate through a fixed list of 3–4 bio templates.
  - Max 2–3 outbound links, and vary WHICH links appear (sometimes Kick+Discord, sometimes Twitch+Kick, sometimes Kick+Twitch+Discord, etc.).
* **EVERY pinned comment must be 100% unique** — no reusable "Click the linked video above" templates. Write a genuine question or observation every time.
* **Tags must be song-specific**, 3–5 tags, no generic tag dumps, no recycling the same tag string across multiple videos.
* **Before writing ANY new optimization, you MUST read the workspace files `Titles.txt` and `Descriptions.txt`** (root of workspace) to verify you are not reusing opening words, verbs, adjectives ("masterpiece", "incredible", "insane"), or structural patterns.
* After completing each new optimization, append the new title and description to `Titles.txt` and `Descriptions.txt` so future checks stay accurate.

**Reference files (live documents — check these before EVERY new write):**
* `Titles.txt` — cumulative database of every long-form + Shorts title ever published, with opening words noted.
* `Descriptions.txt` — cumulative database of every long-form description hook ever published.

## 3. WORKSPACE STRUCTURE & REORGANIZATION
To keep our files, graphics, and video lists organized, the workspace utilizes the following structure:
* `archives/` - Holds initial SEO reports and system audits.
* `sortables/` - Holds all raw pre-filled song templates from the channel backlog as a quick reference base.
* `bands/` - Root folder for all optimized, active band assets.
  * `bands/[0-9, A-Z]/` - Alphabetical band separator folders (e.g., `bands/H/` for Huey Lewis and the News).
    * `bands/[0-9, A-Z]/[Band_Name]/` - Specific band directory (e.g., `bands/H/Huey_Lewis_and_the_News/`).
      * `bands/[0-9, A-Z]/[Band_Name]/[xxxx_Song_Name]/` - Specific song directory utilizing 4-digit unique tracking numbers (e.g., `bands/H/Huey_Lewis_and_the_News/0001_The_Power_of_Love/`).
        * `bands/[0-9, A-Z]/[Band_Name]/[xxxx_Song_Name]/upload/` - Subfolder containing raw uploads, screenshots, and visual assets provided by the user for that specific song.
        * `bands/[0-9, A-Z]/[Band_Name]/[xxxx_Song_Name]/optimization.md` - The specific, finalized SEO file containing the optimized Title, Description, Tags, and Pinned Comment for that song's long-form video and Short.

## 5. INSTRUCTIONS FOR THE NEXT AI ASSISTANT (CARRYOVER PROTOCOL)
If you are starting a new session with MAVO:
1. **Read this file (`MAVO_PROJECT_LOG.txt` or `MAVO_PROJECT_LOG.md`) completely.** It contains all the necessary memory and constraints.
2. Under no circumstances should you duplicate files.
3. If the active `bands/` folders are empty, **extract the REBUILD SCRIPT block at the bottom of the text file, save it as `rebuild_mavo_workspace.py` and run it.** It will instantly rebuild all completed Active folders and `optimization.md` files in less than a second!
4. Check the `sortables/` folder if MAVO gives you a new song name. If a draft folder exists in `sortables/`, **move** it to its respective letter and band inside `bands/`, rename the folder using the `xxxx_Song_Name` 4-digit prefix, and fine-tune its contents based on MAVO's images.
5. Always generate unique, conversational, high-reach titles, descriptions, tag lists, and pinned comments following the **Section 2 Rules**.
6. Document your completed work in **Section 4 (Progress Log)** of this file before finishing a turn.

# Re-create active 20 folders in bands/
if os.path.exists("bands"):
    shutil.rmtree("bands")
os.makedirs("bands", exist_ok=True)
for char in ["0-9"] + [chr(i) for i in range(65, 91)]:
    os.makedirs(f"bands/{char}", exist_ok=True)

# Generate active 20 folders with 100% accurate, highly unique description blocks
for idx, item in enumerate(completed_list, start=1):
    fpath = f"bands/{item['category']}/{item['band'].replace(' ', '_').replace('&amp;', 'and').replace('&', 'and').replace('\'', '')}/{item['folder']}/optimization.md"
    dir_to_make = os.path.dirname(fpath)
    os.makedirs(f"{dir_to_make}/upload", exist_ok=True)
    
    genres = genres_mapping.get(item["band"], "Rock, alternative rock")
    desc_block = about_variations[(idx - 1) % len(about_variations)]
    
    file_content = f"""# {item['band']} - {item['song']} (SEO Optimization)
**Folder Location:** `{dir_to_make}/`
**Wikipedia Genres:** {genres}

---

## 1. LONG-FORM VIDEO SEO PACK

### Title:
```text
{item['lf_title']}
```

### Description:
```text
{item['hook_long']}

{desc_block}

**Genres associated with this video:** {genres}

**Folder Tracking ID:** {item['folder'][:4]}

🎮 Software: Rocksmith 2014 Remastered (on Steam)
🎸 Gear: Epiphone Thunderbird Bass

#BassCover #{item['band'].replace(' ', '').replace('&amp;', 'And').replace('&', 'And').replace('"', '').replace('-', '').replace('.', '').replace('/', '')} #Rocksmith2014 #{item['song'].replace(' ', '').replace('(', '').replace(')', '').replace('!', '').replace(',', '').replace("'", "").replace('"', '').replace('-', '').replace('.', '').replace('/', '')} #EpiphoneThunderbird
"""

### Tags Box:
```text
{item['tags_long']}
```

### Pinned Comment:
```text
{item['pinned_long']}
```

---

## 2. SHORTS VIDEO SEO PACK

```text

```

### Description:
```text

"""
    with open(fpath, "w") as f_opt:
        f_opt.write(file_content)

## 7. DATA-DRIVEN SEO LESSONS FROM YOUTUBE STUDIO ANALYTICS (added 2026-07-14)

After analyzing real performance data from the first 10 published songs (6 screenshots per song = 60 analytics panels from YouTube Studio), these are the concrete takeaways that must guide future optimizations:

### 🔑 Critical Analytics Findings (songs 0005–0010, ~18–24 days post-publish)
| **0045 Chompers Math-Rock Recommend Audit** | 0045L: **6 views — the batch's only ABOVE-usual LF** — on 418 imp (**89.4% recommend-serving = block-high push**), discovery split evenly across Browse/Suggested/search/Related with a 100% gear-demo suggested graph; 0045S: 23 views, 34.8% search, realtime view 100% search, lone term = typo `chomper` 25.0% | — | **YouTube bet big on the math-rock oddball.** The algorithm served this 2012 indie instrumental harder than any other 18-Jun LF (89.4% recommend-serving) and it paid back the block's only above-usual LF view count — eclectic suggested neighbors (guitar rig rundowns, pedalboard demos) are doing identity-free discovery. Searchers meanwhile type the singular `chomper` — typo back-ported to both boxes; `chomper bass cover` compound was already there. 0 pick scrubs. |
| **0044 Galapagos Four-Upload Ecosystem Audit** | Cluster cume ≈ **6,191 SF views** (2307: **3,948 — channel-top-tier SF, +7 subs, 24.9h watch** / 0587S: 1,102 / 0752S: 1,089 / 0044S: 52); 0587L: 3.3k imp @82.7% Browse with a 100% djent-instrumental suggested graph; canonical 0044L AVD 0:50 = block-best LF retention | — | **A niche J-punk deep-cut became the channel's biggest legacy ecosystem.** Every upload of the same song compounded: every suggested panel is 100% Galapagos identity, and search proves the cluster — bare `04 limited sazabys` (66.7% of 0587L search) + `04 limited sazabys live` (7.7% SF, katakana ライブ corollary) + lyric hook `fight so dirty but you love so sweet` now mirrored across all seven tags boxes. **Bug fix:** off-topic `seiji igusa bass` tag was saved in 0044S and farming 7.7% of search — purged. ### 📈 Tag Best Practices (proven by data)
* **Include the bare song name as a tag** (e.g. `juicebox bass`, not just `the strokes juicebox bass cover`). The #1 search term for 0009 was simply "juicebox" (36.4% of search traffic).
* `rocksmith bass` and `rocksmith shorts` reliably appear in search terms across videos — always include one Rocksmith-specific tag.
* Random/noise search terms (`dekho kaise` = Hindi "look how" on 0006, `funny video` on 0009) are YouTube misattribution, NOT tags to add. Do not chase random traffic.
* Keep LF tags at 5, SF tags at 4–5; data shows the current 3–5 tag strategy is not hurting reach — Juicebox pulled 635 views on 5 tags.

### 🎬 Shorts Hook Best Practices (proven by data)
* **First 1 second MUST contain:** (a) the bass riff already playing, (b) your face/reaction visible, (c) NO silent intro / count-in / Rocksmith menu screen.
* 0008 Sacrifice (J-rock, fast riffs) had 0:07 AVD and 81% swipe-away because the fast technical playing didn't land a visual hook fast enough — future fast-riff Shorts need a facecam reaction in frame 1.
* 0010 Band Tee (crunkcore throwback) had BEST retention (31.4%, 0:30 AVD on a 0:36 Short) because the "what am I playing" curiosity + neon crunkcore aesthetic held attention.
* 0009 Juicebox had massive reach (635 views) but only 11.5% retention — trade-off is acceptable (algorithm pushes reachy content hard even with low retention, but adding a 1-sec face hook could push it even higher).

### 🖼️ Long-form Thumbnail Best Practices (proven by data)
* 0010 Band Tee got 3.8% CTR (excellent for bass covers) — replicate that formula: high-contrast face reaction, bold text, eye contact.
* 0006 Dream Weaver's 0.2% CTR means atmospheric/dark thumbnails fail — even though viewers who clicked stayed 1:51, nobody clicked. Use brighter contrast, expressive face, visible Thunderbird.

### 🔬 Batch 0011–0015 Findings (added 2026-07-15, 20-24 days data)
* **Best SF overall:** 0015 Roxanne (Police) — 74 views (+6% above typical), **42.5% stayed** (highest SF retention in channel history!), 0:22 AVD, **80.8% from YouTube search**. Search terms `roxanne` 84.1% dominates. Lesson: iconic-bass-line + bare song-name tag = search-killer. Tag fix applied: added `roxanne bass` + `rocksmith bass` + `sting bass` + `reggae rock bass`; dropped generic `mavo music shorts` + tag-stuffed `roxanne bass cover`.
* **Second-best SF retention:** 0013 Satch Boogie — 36.8% stayed, 0:14 AVD, 42.4% search. Instrumental/shred content retains well when the hook is "can bass survive this?". Tags: added `rocksmith bass` (14.3% search term).
* **Best LF AVD in this batch:** 0014 Hypnotize (SOAD) — **1:09 AVD (+0:41 vs usual!)** despite 0.6% CTR. Suggested videos are great neighbors (Evanescence/BMTH/SOAD Lonely Day/Randy Orton) — the algorithm *wants* to push SOAD, thumb is the bottleneck. SF: 64 views, 54.7% search-driven (largest search share), `hypnotize` 34.3%. SF tags patched to `system of a down bass, hypnotize bass, rocksmith bass, shavo odadjian bass, rocksmith shorts`.
* **Best LF AVD ever:** 0012 No Transitory (Alexisonfire) — **1:14 AVD (+0:22!)** with 57.1% YouTube search. Post-hardcore fans who click STAY. However SF had **worst retention of batch: 12.5% stayed / 0:13 AVD** — same fast-riff-no-hook pattern as 0008 Sacrifice. Lesson repeated: fast post-hardcore/J-rock MUST open with riff+face in frame 1. SF tags added `rocksmith bass` + `post hardcore shorts` (swapped for `mavo music shorts`). Noise term `bhoot ki video` (Hindi) ignored.
* **0011 Last Nite (Strokes):** Middle of the pack — 14 LF views / 1.3% CTR / 0:39 AVD; SF 63 views / 23.3% stayed / 25.4% search. Strokes fans split between Juicebox & Last Nite. SF tags tweaked to bare-song-name `last nite bass` (from `last nite bass cover`) + added `rocksmith bass`.
* **Weird algorithm misfires:** 0013 Satch Boogie LF suggested videos are Ibanez GRG121DX review, Dua Lipa – Break My Heart bass cover, and a Brazilian gospel track. No tag fix can overcome that; the fix is a more Satriani/shred-specific thumbnail so the algorithm learns the audience faster. 0011 LF suggested from an Ibanez review and Dua Lipa as well — indie-rock Strokes content is being mis-clustered with gear reviews. Thumbnail with your face + Thunderbird (not a generic Rocksmith screen) will correct this over time.
* **Tag pattern reinforced across all 5 SFs:** `rocksmith bass` appears as a top-3 search term in EVERY short that got search traffic this batch. It is now a mandatory tag on ALL shorts (alongside the bare song-name bass tag).
* **Noise terms to ignore (continued):** `bhoot ki video` (Hindi "ghost video" — misattribution, same class as `dekho kaise`), `bass tabs for popular songs` (generic, not song-specific), `cover bass` (word-reversal of `bass cover`, covered by our existing tags).

"Club Tropicana" bass cover and 0013 Satch Boogie means the algorithm is clustering 70s/80s pop-rock + shred bass together. That's a *good* neighbor group — keep making 70s/80s pop/rock/glam content (Queen, Alice Cooper, etc.), the algorithm has an audience pocket there.
* **Rule reinforced:** `rocksmith bass` is now on ALL 21 completed SF tags — it showed up as a top search term in 9/10 search-heavy Shorts across batches 0011–0020.
* **Pattern confirmed across ALL 20 published songs:**
  * Search-heavy songs (Police/Queen/Strokes/Hypnotize/Roxanne/Killer Queen) = high views + high retention when bare-song-name tags are used.
  * Feed-heavy songs (Juicebox, Scott Walker) = high reach but lower retention — they need a face-reaction hook in frame 1.
  * Fast-riff songs without face hook (Sacrifice, No Transitory, Machinehead) = 8–19% stayed regardless of tags — HOOK problem, not tag problem.
  * Niche-prog/art-rock songs (Dear Hunter, Dream Weaver) = low views but fans who click STAY — great AVD when they find you; thumbnail remains the blocker.

#### 🔑 Concrete Takeaways for Our 150-Song Repository:
1. **The `rocksmith bass` / `rocksmith` Tag Rule is 100% Validated:** In every single video that generated search terms (`0001 LF`, `0001 SF`, `0002 SF`), `rocksmith` or `rocksmith bass` accounted for **50% to 100% of all search traffic**. All 150 completed song packages in our repository (`0001`–`0150`) have `rocksmith bass` locked into their tag boxes.
2. **Title & Tag Adjustment Audit:** 
   * **Do NOT change titles, descriptions, or tags for `0052`, `0051`, `0001`, or `0002` Shorts or Long-Forms.** The metadata is indexing cleanly, pulling relevant Suggested neighbors (Stranglers, Fleetwood Mac, Bonnie Tyler, Queen), and driving our **4,404 realtime 48-hour views**.
3. **Long-Form CTR Action Plan:** Across all Long-Form videos, impression volume from YouTube's recommendation engine is healthy (**2,100 to 6,600 impressions per video**), but CTR sits between **0.5% and 0.8%**. To turn those thousands of impressions into hundreds of long-form clicks without touching our SEO text, update the **video thumbnails** to include high-contrast bold yellow/white text and MAVO's expressive facecam right next to the Epiphone Thunderbird headstock.

### 🏁 Competitor Visual Blueprint (`@Chainbrain` vs `@MAVOmusic` - Added 2026-07-16)
* **The Diagnosis:** MAVO's current Long-Form thumbnails (`MAVO - new (full).jpeg`) suffer from "Thumbnail Blindness" (`0.5%–0.8% CTR`) because unedited stream captures look identical in the Browse feed.
* **The Competitor Blueprint (`@Chainbrain`):** Uses 50/50 split layouts (Facecam vs Bold Graphic/Typography) on Long-Form, and burns bright text boxes right into the upper safe zone of vertical Shorts frames so mobile uploads always display a bold text hook.
* **MAVO's Implementation Rules:**
  1. **Long-Form Rule:** Never upload raw stream captures. Use our `1920x1080` custom placeholders (`thumbnail_LF_placeholder.jpg`) or 50/50 split layouts (`MAVO_Concept_1..3.jpg`) pairing bold yellow/white typography (`"175 BPM DNB METAL"`, `"SYNTH TO STRINGS"`) alongside an expressive Thunderbird/Facecam shot.
  2. **Shorts Mobile Upload Rule:** Burn a `10px` gold-bordered or neon-outlined text box (`"🔥 175 BPM PICK STAMINA TEST 🔥"`) into the top/upper-middle third of your vertical video frames during editing (`MAVO_Concept_4_ShortsInVideoHook.jpg`). This completely bypasses YouTube's mobile Shorts thumbnail restrictions.

### 📚 Workspace File Reference (always check before writing)
* `Titles.txt` — every LF + SF title with opening word noted
* `Descriptions.txt` — every LF + SF description hook
* `Pinned_Comments.txt` — every LF + SF pinned comment
* `Tags.txt` — every LF + SF tag set (built from YouTube Studio search-term data)
* `bands/X/Artist/xxxx_Song/optimization.md` — canonical source of truth for each song
* `bands/X/Artist/xxxx_Song/upload/` — all raw screenshots, thumbnails, visual assets for that song

## 8. FULL WORKSPACE RECOVERY PROTOCOL (for new chat sessions)
If you are starting a fresh chat and only have this log file, follow these steps to fully restore the workspace:

1. Copy the Python script from Section 6 → save as `rebuild_mavo_workspace.py` → run it (this bootstraps the 20 completed songs' folders/optimization.md files).
2. **IMPORTANT:** The Section 6 script produces content with minor repetition issues (4-way bio rotation, shared openers). After running it, immediately apply the ZERO REPETITION rules from Section 2's 🚨 rule and regenerate:
   - Rewrite every bio/CTA block fresh (no rotation templates)
   - Rotate the gear/software footer line 20 different ways
   - Remove any `**Tracking ID:**` line from public description text
   - Ensure LF titles open with 20 unique first words
   - Ensure SF titles open with 20 unique first non-hashtag words
   - Ensure LF description hooks open with 20 unique verbs/phrasings
   - Ensure SF descriptions open with 20 unique phrases
   - Ensure LF pinned comments open with unique phrases
   - Ensure SF pinned comments open with unique phrases
3. Create these four cumulative reference files at the workspace root (or rebuild them by parsing all `optimization.md` files):
   - `Titles.txt` (LF + SF titles with opening words)
   - `Descriptions.txt` (LF + SF description hooks)
   - `Pinned_Comments.txt` (LF + SF pinned comments)
   - `Tags.txt` (LF + SF tag sets)
4. When MAVO provides YouTube Studio screenshots, save them to the matching `bands/X/Artist/xxxx_Song/upload/` folder and read the "YouTube search terms" and "Audience retention" panels — use them to tune tags and hook strategy per Section 7.
5. After every new optimization:
   - Write the `optimization.md` file
   - Copy any new screenshots into `upload/`
   - Append new titles/descriptions/pins/tags to the four reference .txt files
   - Update Section 4 progress-log row with final title choices
   - If data reveals a new SEO lesson, add it to Section 7 with date

### Quick verification bash command (run after any rebuild):
```bash
cd /home/user && python3 -c "
import os,re
from collections import Counter
def audit(label, items, key=lambda x:x.split()[0].lower().lstrip('#').rstrip(':')):
    c=Counter(key(x) for x in items if x.split())
    d={k:v for k,v in c.items() if v>1}
    print(f'{label:35s}: {"ALL UNIQUE ✓" if not d else "DUPS: "+str(d)}')
lf_t,sf_t,lf_d,sf_d,lf_p,sf_p=[],[],[],[],[],[]
for root,_,files in os.walk('bands'):
    for fn in files:
        if fn!='optimization.md': continue
        t=open(os.path.join(root,fn)).read()
        def g(sec,lab):
            m=re.search(rf'### {lab}:\n\`\`\`text\n(.*?)\n\`\`\`',sec,re.S)
            return m.group(1).strip() if m else ''
        parts=t.split('## 2. SHORTS VIDEO SEO PACK')
        lf=parts[0]; sf=parts[1] if len(parts)>1 else ''
        if g(lf,'Title'): lf_t.append(g(lf,'Title'))
        if g(sf,'Title'): sf_t.append(g(sf,'Title'))
        if g(lf,'Description'): lf_d.append(g(lf,'Description').split(chr(10))[0])
        if g(sf,'Description'): sf_d.append(g(sf,'Description').split(chr(10))[0])
        if g(lf,'Pinned Comment'): lf_p.append(g(lf,'Pinned Comment'))
        if g(sf,'Pinned Comment'): sf_p.append(g(sf,'Pinned Comment'))
audit('Long-form titles (1st word)', lf_t)
audit('Shorts titles (1st word)', sf_t)
audit('Long-form desc hooks (1st word)', lf_d)
audit('Shorts desc (1st word)', sf_d)
audit('Long-form pins (1st word)', lf_p)
audit('Shorts pins (1st word)', sf_p)
"
```
All six lines should say "ALL UNIQUE ✓". If any show DUPS, fix them before continuing.

### 9.2 🚨 ZERO REPETITION / INAUTHENTIC-CONTENT BYPASS RULES (MANDATORY)

YouTube 2025–2026 'Inauthentic Content / repetition' detector flags **semantic repetition**, not just byte-identical strings. These rules override everything:

1. **Every LF title must open with a unique first word.** No repeating opener verbs ("Laying down", "Tackling", "Cranking" are the old banned rotation).
2. **Every SF title must open with a unique first word** — different pool from LF titles.
3. **Every LF description's first 2–3 lines must be unique** — same sentence skeleton with swapped song/artist counts as repetition.
4. **Every SF description's first line must be unique.**
5. **Bio/CTA block must be freshly written every video** — NO 3–4 template rotation. Every paragraph of self-intro must differ.
6. **Max 2–3 outbound links per video**, rotated across Kick/Twitch/Discord. Don't paste all 3 every time.
7. **Pinned comments must be unique conversational questions**, never "Click the linked video above!" templates, never include links.
8. **Gear/software footer line** ("Epiphone Thunderbird … / Rocksmith 2014 Remastered …") must be MICRO-VARIED every video — never byte-identical.
9. **NO tracking IDs (xxxx song IDs) in any public-facing text** — IDs live only in folder paths and internal filenames.
10. **Hashtags:** 5 per LF description, 4 per SF description; no copy-paste hashtag groups.
11. **Tags:** 3–5 per video, song-specific. ALWAYS include a bare song-name tag (e.g. `juicebox bass`, `roxanne bass` — proven by analytics to be the highest-search driver). **`rocksmith bass` is mandatory on every Short** (top-3 search term in 9/10 search-heavy Shorts).
12. **`**Genres:**` line** is mandatory in every LF description (genres from Wikipedia/AllMusic).
13. **Shorts hook (data-proven):** Frame 1 must contain (a) riff already playing, (b) MAVO's face/reaction visible, (c) NO silent intro / count-in / Rocksmith menu screen. Fast riffs (J-rock/post-hardcore/grunge) without face in frame 1 = 8–19% swipe-away death (0008 Sacrifice, 0012 No Transitory, 0019 Machinehead all proved this).
14. **LF thumbnail best practice:** bright contrast + face reaction + visible Thunderbird. Dark/atmospheric thumbs = 0.2% CTR death (0006 Dream Weaver proved it) even when AVD is excellent.
15. **Pre-write checklist:** BEFORE writing any new optimization.md, read `Titles.txt` + `Descriptions.txt` + `Pinned_Comments.txt` to confirm opener/phrase uniqueness.
16. **After every write:** (a) save optimization.md, (b) copy any new user screenshots to that song's `upload/` folder, (c) rebuild the four reference files (Titles.txt, Descriptions.txt, Pinned_Comments.txt, Tags.txt) by reading ALL canonical optimization.md files, (d) post changed fields in one-click-copy ```text code blocks so user can paste directly into YouTube, (e) update Section 4 progress-log row in this log.
17. **Noise search terms to IGNORE** (do not add as tags; they are misattributions): `dekho kaise` (Hindi), `bhoot ki video` (Hindi), `funny video` (vibe tag, not target), `cartoon cartoon cartoon cartoon` (random misattribution), `bass tabs for popular songs` (too generic), `cover bass` (word-reversal already covered by `bass cover`).

def write_thumbnail(s, upload_dir):
    try:
        import PIL
        from PIL import Image, ImageDraw, ImageFont
    except Exception as e:
        return
    out_img_path = os.path.join(upload_dir, 'thumbnail_LF_placeholder.jpg')
    if os.path.exists(out_img_path):
        return out_img_path
    try:
        font_xl = ImageFont.truetype("/usr/share/fonts/truetype/dejavu/DejaVuSans-Bold.ttf", 100)
        font_lg = ImageFont.truetype("/usr/share/fonts/truetype/dejavu/DejaVuSans-Bold.ttf", 78)
        font_md = ImageFont.truetype("/usr/share/fonts/truetype/dejavu/DejaVuSans-Bold.ttf", 54)
        font_sm = ImageFont.truetype("/usr/share/fonts/truetype/dejavu/DejaVuSans-Bold.ttf", 40)
    except:
        font_xl = font_lg = font_md = font_sm = ImageFont.load_default()

    def draw_text_outlined(draw, pos, text, font, fill_color, outline_color="#000000", outline_width=6):
        x, y = pos
        for dx in range(-outline_width, outline_width + 1):
            for dy in range(-outline_width, outline_width + 1):
                if dx*dx + dy*dy <= outline_width*outline_width:
                    draw.text((x + dx, y + dy), text, font=font, fill=outline_color)
        draw.text((x, y), text, font=font, fill=fill_color)

    g = s.get('genres', '').lower()
    if any(k in g for k in ['death', 'black metal', 'thrash', 'deathcore', 'extreme', 'horror']):
        top_c, bot_c = (60, 5, 10), (15, 5, 8)
    elif any(k in g for k in ['metalcore', 'nu metal', 'industrial', 'neue deutsche']):
        top_c, bot_c = (15, 45, 20), (10, 15, 12)
    elif any(k in g for k in ['pop', 'electropop', 'dance', 'disco', 'synth', 'new wave']):
        top_c, bot_c = (45, 10, 60), (12, 8, 25)
    elif any(k in g for k in ['grunge', 'alternative', 'indie', 'garage', 'punk', 'emo']):
        top_c, bot_c = (55, 30, 5), (15, 10, 8)
    elif any(k in g for k in ['drum and bass', 'dubstep', 'electronic', 'psytrance', 'rave']):
        top_c, bot_c = (10, 35, 65), (8, 12, 25)
    else:
        top_c, bot_c = (50, 40, 10), (15, 15, 15)

    im = Image.new('RGB', (1920, 1080), bot_c)
    draw = ImageDraw.Draw(im)
    for y in range(1080):
        ratio = y / 1080.0
        r = int(top_c[0] * (1 - ratio) + bot_c[0] * ratio)
        g_val = int(top_c[1] * (1 - ratio) + bot_c[1] * ratio)
        b = int(top_c[2] * (1 - ratio) + bot_c[2] * ratio)
        draw.line([(0, y), (1920, y)], fill=(r, g_val, b))

    artist_upper = s['artist'].upper()
    song_upper = s['song'].upper()
    draw.rectangle([(60, 50), (620, 120)], fill="#E60000", outline="#FFFFFF", width=3)
    draw_text_outlined(draw, (85, 60), "ROCKSMITH 2014 BASS", font_sm, "#FFFFFF", outline_width=3)
    f_artist = font_lg if len(artist_upper) > 16 else font_xl
    f_song = font_md if len(song_upper) > 18 else font_lg
    draw_text_outlined(draw, (65, 170), artist_upper, f_artist, "#FFD700", outline_width=8)
    draw_text_outlined(draw, (65, 290), f'"{song_upper}"', f_song, "#FFFFFF", outline_width=7)
    genre_badge = s.get('genres', 'Bass').split(',')[0].upper() + " BASS COVER"
    draw_text_outlined(draw, (65, 430), genre_badge, font_md, "#00FFFF", outline_width=5)
    draw_text_outlined(draw, (65, 960), "EPIPHONE THUNDERBIRD • 100% UNIQUE CUT", font_sm, "#FFD700", outline_width=4)
    
    frame_left, frame_top, frame_right, frame_bot = 1220, 130, 1860, 950
    draw.rectangle([(frame_left-6, frame_top-6), (frame_right+6, frame_bot+6)], fill="#111111", outline="#FFD700", width=10)
    draw_text_outlined(draw, (frame_left + 110, frame_top + 320), "[ FACE CAM ]", font_md, "#FFD700", outline_width=4)
    draw_text_outlined(draw, (frame_left + 85, frame_top + 410), "PLACEHOLDER", font_md, "#FFFFFF", outline_width=4)
    draw_text_outlined(draw, (frame_left + 75, frame_top + 520), "(Overlay photo on day off)", font_sm, "#AAAAAA", outline_width=3)
    
    im.save(out_img_path, quality=90)
    return out_img_path

def write_optimization_md(s):
    s = {k: (v.encode("utf-16", "surrogatepass").decode("utf-16") if isinstance(v, str) else v) for k, v in s.items()}
    letter_dir = os.path.join(BANDS, s['letter'])
    os.makedirs(letter_dir, exist_ok=True)
    artist_folder = s['artist_folder']
    song_dir = os.path.join(letter_dir, artist_folder, s['folder'])
    upload_dir = os.path.join(song_dir, 'upload')
    os.makedirs(upload_dir, exist_ok=True)
    write_thumbnail(s, upload_dir)
    L = []
    L.append(f"# {s['artist']} - {s['song']} (SEO Optimization)")
    L.append(f"**Folder Location:** `bands/{s['letter']}/{s['artist_folder']}/{s['folder']}/`")
    L.append(f"**Wikipedia Genres:** {s['genres']}")
    L.append("")
    L.append("---")
    L.append("")
    L.append("## 1. LONG-FORM VIDEO SEO PACK")
    L.append("")
    L.append("### Title:")
    L.append("```text")
    L.append(s['lf_title'])
    L.append("```")
    L.append("")
    L.append("### Description:")
    L.append("```text")
    L.append(s['lf_desc'])
    L.append("```")
    L.append("")
    L.append("### Tags Box:")
    L.append("```text")
    L.append(s['lf_tags'])
    L.append("```")
    L.append("")
    L.append("### Pinned Comment:")
    L.append("```text")
    L.append(s['lf_pin'])
    L.append("```")
    L.append("")
    L.append("---")
    L.append("")
    L.append("## 2. SHORTS VIDEO SEO PACK")
    L.append("")
    L.append("### Title:")
    L.append("```text")
    L.append(s['sf_title'])
    L.append("```")
    L.append("")
    L.append("### Description:")
    L.append("```text")
    L.append(s['sf_desc'])
    L.append("```")
    L.append("")
    L.append("### Tags Box:")
    L.append("```text")
    L.append(s['sf_tags'])
    L.append("```")
    L.append("")
    L.append("### Pinned Comment:")
    L.append("```text")
    L.append(s['sf_pin'])
    L.append("```")
    
    out_path = os.path.join(song_dir, 'optimization.md')
    with open(out_path, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:
        f.write('\n'.join(L) + '\n')
    return out_path

def rebuild_refs():
    files = glob.glob(os.path.join(BANDS, '**', 'optimization.md'), recursive=True)
    files = sorted(files)
    t_out = open(os.path.join(ROOT, 'Titles.txt'), 'w', encoding='utf-8')
    d_out = open(os.path.join(ROOT, 'Descriptions.txt'), 'w', encoding='utf-8')
    p_out = open(os.path.join(ROOT, 'Pinned_Comments.txt'), 'w', encoding='utf-8')
    g_out = open(os.path.join(ROOT, 'Tags.txt'), 'w', encoding='utf-8')
    
    n = 0
    for fpath in files:
        with open(fpath, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:
            content = f.read()
        
        parts = re.split(r'## 2\. SHORTS', content, maxsplit=1)
        lf_part = parts[0]
        sf_part = parts[1] if len(parts)>1 else ""
            
        def extract(lab, text):
            pat = rf'### {re.escape(lab)}:\s*\n```text\s*\n(.*?)\n```'
            m = re.search(pat, text, re.DOTALL)
            if m: return m.group(1).strip()
            pat2 = rf'### {re.escape(lab)}:\s*\n```text\s*\n(.*?)```'
            m = re.search(pat2, text, re.DOTALL)
            return m.group(1).strip() if m else ""

        lf_t = extract("Title", lf_part) or extract("Title Box", lf_part)
        sf_t = extract("Title", sf_part) or extract("Title Box", sf_part)
        
        lf_d = extract("Description", lf_part) or extract("Description Box", lf_part)
        sf_d = extract("Description", sf_part) or extract("Description Box", sf_part)
        
        lf_p = extract("Pinned Comment", lf_part) or extract("Pinned Comment Box", lf_part)
        sf_p = extract("Pinned Comment", sf_part) or extract("Pinned Comment Box", sf_part)
        
        lf_g = extract("Tags", lf_part) or extract("Tags Box", lf_part)
        sf_g = extract("Tags", sf_part) or extract("Tags Box", sf_part)
        
        folder = os.path.basename(os.path.dirname(fpath))
        header = f"[{folder}]\n"
        
        t_out.write(header + f"LF: {lf_t}\nSF: {sf_t}\n\n")
        d_out.write(header + f"LF:\n{lf_d}\n\nSF:\n{sf_d}\n\n")
        p_out.write(header + f"LF: {lf_p}\nSF: {sf_p}\n\n")
        g_out.write(header + f"LF: {lf_g}\nSF: {sf_g}\n\n")
        n += 1

    for f in (t_out, d_out, p_out, g_out): f.close()
    return n

if __name__ == '__main__':
    for s in SONGS:
        d = write_optimization_md(s)
        print(f"Wrote {s['id']} -> {d}")
    n = rebuild_refs()
    print(f"Reference files rebuilt. Total songs: {n}")
```

### 9.6 Verification (run after ANY write — all seven lines must say 'all unique ✓')

```bash
cd /home/user && python3 -c "
import re, glob, collections
files = sorted(glob.glob('bands/**/optimization.md', recursive=True))
lf_o=[]; sf_o=[]; lfd1=[]; sfd1=[]; lfp=[]; sfp=[]; gears=[]
for f in files:
    t = open(f).read()
    def g(lab,src):
        p = r'### ' + re.escape(lab) + r':\s*\n\`\`\`text\s*\n(.*?)\n\`\`\`'
        m = re.search(p, src, re.DOTALL)
        if m: return m.group(1).rstrip()
        p2 = r'### ' + re.escape(lab) + r':\s*\n\`\`\`text\s*\n(.*?)\`\`\`'
        m2 = re.search(p2, src, re.DOTALL)
        return m2.group(1).rstrip() if m2 else ''
    parts = t.split('## 2. SHORTS',1)
    lf=parts[0]; sf=parts[1] if len(parts)>1 else ''
    lft,sft = g('Title',lf), g('Title',sf)
    lfd,sfd = g('Description',lf), g('Description',sf)
    lfp.append(g('Pinned Comment',lf)); sfp.append(g('Pinned Comment',sf))
    lf_o.append(re.match(r'([A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9!?\-]*)',lft).group(1))
    sf_o.append(re.match(r'([A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9!?\-]*)',sft).group(1))
    lfd1.append(lfd.splitlines()[0].strip()); sfd1.append(sfd.splitlines()[0].strip())
    gm = re.search(r'\*\*Genres:\*\*.*\n(.+?Rocksmith.+?Thunderbird.*)',t)
    if gm: gears.append(gm.group(1).strip())
def audit(n,items):
    d = [x for x,c in collections.Counter(items).items() if c>1]
    print(f'{n}: {len(items)} -> {"DUPES: "+str(d) if d else "all unique ✓"}')
audit('LF openers',lf_o); audit('SF openers',sf_o); audit('LF desc first line',lfd1)
audit('SF desc first line',sfd1); audit('LF pins',lfp); audit('SF pins',sfp); audit('Gear lines',gears)
"
```

### 9.9 Ongoing Workflow
4. **CHECKPOINT RULE:** Every 5 songs (0005, 0010, 0015, 0020, 0025, 0030, etc.), explicitly review the log file to ensure no global rules or insights have been missed. Mention doing this check in the response.

1. User pastes analytics screenshots in batches of 5 songs (e.g. 0005–0010, 0011–0015, 0016–0020). File them into each song's `upload/` folder (named like `NN - N.PNG` as provided).
2. Per batch: analyze search terms + retention + traffic source. Apply tag edits only where search terms demand them. Do NOT change titles/descriptions unless user requests or CTR is catastrophically bad AND a clear alternative is data-supported.
3. After each tag edit: rebuild all four reference files; post one-click copy blocks; update Section 7 analytics findings.
4. When user says "optimize NNNN", follow ZERO REPETITION rules: read Titles.txt + Descriptions.txt + Pinned_Comments.txt first, write full unique SEO pack, save optimization.md, rebuild refs, post copy blocks, update Section 4 row.
5. Next up when user says go: **0022 – The Clash – Guns of Brixton** (then 0023–0050 in order).

### 9.10 Data Lessons (concise reference)
* Typo capture works: 0021 Good God search traffic was 100% "good good" (from original log typo); capturing natural typos in tags brings views.

* Iconic-bass-line songs with face-in-thumb + bare-song-name tag win search (Roxanne 42.5%/80.8% search, Killer Queen 37.9%/61.3%, Juicebox 635 views/95% feed).
* Fast-riff songs (J-rock/post-hardcore/grunge) MUST have face reaction in frame 1 or they die at 8–19% retention (0008, 0012, 0019).
* Dark/atmospheric LF thumbnails kill CTR even when AVD is excellent (0006 Dream Weaver 0.2% CTR / 1:51 AVD).
* 70s/80s pop-rock/glam content clusters well in suggested videos (0018 Queen suggested alongside Wham!/Satch Boogie — make more).
* Niche-prog/art-rock has small but loyal audience (0016 Dear Hunter 35.7% SF stayed, 0:26 AVD, 43.8% search).
* Algorithm misclusters sometimes (Satch Boogie being suggested by Ibanez review + Dua Lipa + Brazilian gospel); fix is thumbnail specificity, not tags.
* Search-term data shows `rocksmith bass` appears in top 3 search terms for 9/10 Shorts that got any search traffic — mandatory tag.
* `rocksmith bass` > `rocksmith shorts` for search reach; keep both only when there's room (5-tag cap).

## 11. STRICT WORKSPACE ORGANIZATION RULESET (MANDATORY FOR ALL AI AGENTS)
To keep the workspace clean, professional, and uncluttered, the following file placement rules must NEVER be violated by any AI agent:
1. **Root Directory (`/home/user/`):** ONLY `.txt` documentation/reference files (`Titles.txt`, `Descriptions.txt`, `Pinned_Comments.txt`, `Tags.txt`, `MAVO_PROJECT_LOG.txt`, `MAVO_VERSION_LOG.txt`, `Master_Long_Form_Repository_0001_0702.txt`, `Master_Long_Form_Backlog_151_702.txt`, `Cleaned_Long_Form_Videos.txt`, `CONTEXT_BLOCK.txt`), `.zip` master archives (`MAVOmusic_Master_Restoration_Package_v1.08.zip`), and `.sh` verification scripts (`verify.sh`) are permitted inside the root folder.
2. **Python Scripts (`PY/`):** ALL `.py` files (`recover_mavo.py`, `verify.py`, batch scripts, log updaters) MUST be saved inside `/home/user/PY/`. Never leave `.py` files in the root.
3. **Images (`IMAGES/`):** ALL standalone image files (`.jpg`, `.jpeg`, `.png`) generated or tested during workflows MUST be saved inside `/home/user/IMAGES/` (unless they specifically belong inside a song's `bands/[Letter]/[Artist]/[ID]_[Song]/upload/` directory or `archives/competitor_analysis/`).
4. **Canonical SEO Packs (`bands/`):** Every completed song's `optimization.md` file and `upload/` assets reside inside its exact `bands/[Letter]/[Artist]/[ID]_[Song]/` folder.
5. **Archives (`archives/`):** Historical assets and competitor screenshots reside in `archives/competitor_analysis/`.

## 12. GOLDEN TAG SYNC & ANALYTICS CROSS-CHECK
When overhauling matched Shorts (alternate cuts), always audit the provided YouTube Studio screenshots. ## 13. HIERARCHICAL WORKSPACE ARCHITECTURE
All song assets are organized by song title first, then by ID:
`bands/[Letter]/[Artist]/[Song]/[ID]/[DD-MM-YYYY].md`
This structure ensures that multiple cuts of the same song are grouped logically. The root directory must remain clean of non-documentation files.

## 14. YOUTUBE STUDIO SCREENSHOT AUDIT PROTOCOL (NNNNL / NNNNS)
When fine-tuning canonical items `0001`–`0150` via uploaded YouTube Studio screenshots:
1. **Naming Convention:**
   - `NNNNL - 1`: Long-form "Since published" date snippet.
   - `NNNNL - 2, 3, 4`: Main long-form YouTube Studio analytics/search snippets.
   - `NNNNS - 1`: Short-form "Since published" date snippet.
   - `NNNNS - 2, 3, 4`: Main short-form YouTube Studio analytics/search snippets.
2. **Reporting Output Format:**
   - If updates/Golden Tags are found: Report affected asset and present each updated field (Title, Description, Genre, Tags, Pinned Comment) in single copyable code blocks.
   - If no changes are required: Output strictly `[NNNN] - no changes.` to conserve tokens and execution time.
3. **Restoration & ZIP Backup Maintenance:**
   - Keep root clean (only `CONTEXT_BLOCK.txt`, `MAVO_PROJECT_LOG.txt`, `MAVO_VERSION_LOG.txt`, `Matched_Shorts_To_Cross_Check.txt`).
   - Store all `.txt` files in `TXT/`, scripts in `PY/`, images in `IMAGES/`, and backups in `ZIP/` (max 2 latest version zips kept).

[VER 3.4 - 2026-11-01] LATEST-UPLOAD WAVE AUDIT (26-28 Aug 2026, 12 Shorts + 12 LFs) - TITLE STYLE KEPT BESPOKE, MONETISATION HYPOTHESIS CORRECTED. MAVO pushed back on the VER 3.2 keyword rule ("breaking the original idea back to band and song will create repetitions") and asked for the latest uploads to be analysed first. All 24 rows map to packages 0093/0095/0096/0099/0100/0101/0102/0103/0104/0111/0124 and every LF/SF title byte-matches its package. **ANSWER: NO, we do not go back to `Artist - Song`.** The bespoke Shorts averaged **1,320 views** (1,003-1,904) in 1-3 days, above the 790-1,100 typical band, and 3 of the 12 carry neither artist nor song in the title - wording is not the constraint. The same shipment's long-forms are 12/12 "Ineligible to earn / Ads off" (4 "Partially blocked") at an average of 72 views and GBP 0.00, while 12/12 Shorts earned GBP 0.04-0.17. **So the split is FORMAT-driven, not title-driven.** New S32 contract: bespoke voice + natural mention of artist/song (title, or first description line/hashtags) + one format token on long-form (bass cover / playthrough / Rocksmith); the dash template is explicitly banned again. **S33 correction:** the CDLC-link theory is DOWNGRADED - the Aug packs have no links of any kind and are still blocked, so the block is claim-driven (E4-a), not policy-driven; "Shopping disabled" on Shorts is product tagging, not an ads block. Recreation doctrine corrected: do not spend sessions re-recording LFs that will simply re-claim; remake only E1/E2 or a genuinely different audio bed. **Fixed:** 11 packages date-synced to the real Studio dates (were 17-07/07-08/08-08); track history gained per-ID Last Checked support and now marks the 11 audited packages 01-11-2026. **Missing:** Chevelle has no package anywhere (SF 1,123 / LF 98, 26-08-26) - need the song name. **Edits:** CO-0002 stands as written (already house pattern); CO-0001 title rewritten into house style.

[VER 3.5 - 2026-11-01] TYPOGRAPHY SWEEP (ASCII ONLY) + HOUSE FORMULA CODIFIED + CO-0001/CO-0002 FINAL COPY. MAVO: "NOT use en dashes or anything that can lead AI to read that it is AI SEO. Use - instead of —." The audit found this was not a two-file issue but a corpus-wide AI tell: **184 of 308 packages carried 408 em dashes**, plus 2 in the monetisation register and 2 in the kit guide. All 408 were replaced with hyphens (0 remaining) and Tier-1 verify.py now FAILS the build on `—` `–` `’` `‘` `“` `”` `…` in any package, so it cannot creep back in. **PATTERN DECISION:** the draft "Motionless In White's Another Life bass cover - metalcore's saddest song" is REJECTED - it is a fixed shape, so reusing it would break the uniqueness rule and banning it later would be inconsistent with what we already shipped. S32 now defines the house formula: a third-person bespoke claim about the bass/bassist with artist and song named naturally inside the sentence, the word "bass" present, and the structure varied every time; the literal phrase "bass cover" is downgraded from mandatory to optional because it would itself become a template. Reference set = the 26-28 Aug wave titles. FINAL COPY approved and gated: CO-0001 "Burzum's 'Autumn Leaves' stretched into 1 hour of calm dungeon synth" (68ch) and CO-0002 "Motionless In White's 'Another Life' hides one of metalcore's softest basslines!" (80ch), both with ASCII descriptions and pins. Chevelle - The Red is confirmed as the missing package (CO-0003 candidate, queued).

VER 3.6 - 2026-11-01 - FORMAT-LEVEL DECISION: THE PLAY ALONG SERIES IS KEPT
================================================================================
MAVO uploaded 10 panels of _All Songs/0003 - Motionless In White - Masterpiece
(Long Form - Play Along on Bass) with a question that was not about the song:
"1 of 8 play along recording. This is just showcasing the game with full song on
screen without me playing it. Shall we keep these or remove them?"

This is the first FORMAT-level question in the walk. Every previous entry judged
a song: its title, its description, its tags, its pin. This one asks whether a
whole content type deserves to exist. It cannot be answered by reading the copy
sitting on one video, so it was answered from the video's own analytics.

THE EVIDENCE
  790 views, 27.2 h watch time, +51 subscribers.
  Search    71.1% of traffic, and the search terms are real song queries
            ("motionless in white masterpiece cover", "rocksmith bass drop d",
            "rocksmith bass metal") rather than the channel's own name.
  CTR       9.3% on 6.4k impressions - high, and high on a machine-shaped title.
  Retention 56.2% average viewed, 70% still watching at 0:30, above the
            channel's typical curve.
  Audience  86.6% not subscribed. 459 unique viewers. UK 3.7%.
  Devices   TV 34.7%, mobile 34.2%, computer 28.7%. TV-first viewing is what a
            play along is for: the set gets the big screen and the player plays.
  Series    Viewers-also-watch carries the sibling MIW play alongs at 989, 510,
            356 and 333 views, next to a 37-view MIW Short. The format clusters
            its audience; the Short does not.
  Money     0.00. Ineligible to earn. "Nothing to show" on the Revenue card.

THE DECISION
  KEEP THE FORMAT. RETIRE THE SHELL. Do not delete, do not leave as-is.
  This is the clearest traffic-versus-earnings gap in the corpus. The reach is
  near the best in the channel; the return is zero; and the gap is ELIGIBILITY,
  not demand. Removing the video would remove an indexed search entry point that
  converts roughly one in nine of its viewers into a subscriber. Leaving it alone
  locks in a six-block bracketed template with a live "[CDLC DOWNLOAD LINK] SOON"
  placeholder, which is the leading policy suspect in the whole archive.

WHY THE CORPUS ALREADY AGREED
  Section C of the master repository is titled "PLAYALONG & LOOP ARCHIVE
  (UNNUMBERED / NO SHORTS OR OVERHAUL REQUIRED)". Masterpiece is [PA/LOOP 08].
  Section D, which is the removal list, holds exactly one item and it is not a
  play along. A deletion would contradict a decision already recorded in the
  workspace. Section C items are package-exempt, so no bands/ entry was created.

THE HONESTY PROBLEM, AND WHY THE COPY IS WRITTEN DIFFERENTLY
  Every other file in the corpus is voiced as a performed cover: "How did the
  drive hold up?", "Did the stomp get your fist in the air?". On this video MAVO
  is not playing. Copy in that voice would ask a viewer to review a take that
  does not exist - and a viewer who works that out is a viewer who leaves. The
  new copy sells what the video actually is: the full song, the bass part on
  screen, no overdubs, useful to learn from and fine to just listen to.

DELIVERED
  Title    Every bass note in Motionless In White's 'Masterpiece', left on screen
           (70 chars, opener "Every" used 0 times across 308 packages)
  Desc     1,034 chars, all six bracketed blocks and the CDLC link deleted with
           no replacement, rebuilt to the 26-28 Aug wave rhythm
  Tags     20 tags / 362 chars, down from 27 / 498. Generic filler (games,
           streamer, twitch, dlc, cdlc, how to play) and the "cdlc" tag dropped;
           the three phrases the analytics actually prove promoted.
  Pin      one question, 119 chars, zero URLs. The dead 2022 schedule block is
           gone - a 2022 weekly schedule on a 2022 upload reads as abandoned.

NEW RULE (§34)
  Judge a format on discovery, retention and subscriber conversion SEPARATELY
  from earning eligibility. A format is never removed for returning GBP 0.00
  while it is still out-performing on search, retention and conversion. Fix the
  badge and the shell first; only delete when the analytics themselves go quiet.

NEXT
  The CDLC-link cheap test now runs on THIS video rather than CO-0002: it has
  more traffic, the placeholder is still live, and one answer applies to the
  whole series. Strip the link, save, re-read the badge at 48-72 h.
  Roll-out across [PA/LOOP 01-09] waits for MAVO to confirm the count - he says
  8, the archive holds 7 PLAYALONG plus 2 LOOP (Chopin, Burzum).
  Chevelle renumbered CO-0004.

VER 3.7 - 2026-11-01 - THE FIRST EARNING LONG-FORM, AND WHY IT EARNS
================================================================================
MAVO sent a single Studio screen covering the whole play along series and said
"8 Play alongs. We not dealing with chevelle at the moment."

COUNT SETTLED AT 8. MAVO was right, my earlier read was wrong: Section C files 7
PLAYALONG plus 2 LOOP, so it was never a complete count - the missing eighth is
Another Life, which Section C does not classify as a play along.

CONFIRMED LIVE: the CO-0003 retitle is applied. 26-10-2022 (not 25-10 as first
recorded), 790 views, still Ineligible / Ads off. The badge was never going to
move on metadata alone; the retitle buys the search door, not the money.

THE EIGHT: 790 Masterpiece, 989 Another Life, 356 Abigail, 333 America, 510
Immaculate Misconception, 407 X-Kid (partially blocked), 504 Black Parade
(partially blocked), 1,347 .38 Special. Total 5,236 views. Total revenue 0.00.

THE NINTH ROW IS THE FIND
  Frederic Chopin - Nocturne In Eb Major, Op. 9, No. 2 [1 HOUR LOOP]
  03-11-2022 | 4,887 views | "Sharing ad earnings" | GBP 0.54 | 9 comments
First earning long-form in the corpus. Logged MR-0004. The section 33 WIN
CONDITION is met.

WHY IT EARNS - AND WHAT IT BREAKS
Chopin died in 1849. The composition is public domain, the audio bed is
unclaimed, Content ID has nothing to match. Eight play alongs earn nothing; one
classical loop, same channel, same month, worse description, earns 0.54.
  - It kills "long-form cannot earn on this channel". That was only ever tested
    on claimed modern catalogues.
  - It kills "the description link wall causes E4". This video carries a Twitch
    link, a Discord invite and a text block, and monetises anyway.
  - The CDLC placeholder test survives, but its expected value drops sharply.
Rate per 1,000 views, computable for the first time:
  public-domain loop  0.1105
  Aug Shorts wave     0.0777
  Aug long-form wave  0.0000

NEW SECTION 35 - THE REPERTOIRE RULE
Rights beat metadata. Long-form income does not come from re-recording claimed
covers better; that door is closed by copyright, not by effort. It comes from
pointing the long-form lane at unclaimed repertoire: the pre-1900 canon,
traditional, folk, hymn, and anything genuinely unadministered.

HONESTY ABOUT THE ROLL-OUT
Fixing the remaining 7 play alongs is a TRAFFIC project, not a revenue project.
They are claimed compositions and will stay ineligible however good the copy is.
Do them for 5,236 views of free search traffic and the subscriber conversion, and
never imply a badge flip that is not coming.

Also flagged: Another Life exists twice - CO-0002 at 224 views (01-10-2022) and
the play along at 989 views (26-10-2022). Do not cross-paste the rewrites.

NEXT: play along 2 (Another Life, 989 views) - results incoming.

  CO-0002  01-10-2022  3:44   224 views  - already rewritten

"Content suggesting this video" reads 24.6% for "Every bass note in Motionless In
White's 'Master..." - the Masterpiece retitle, live only days. It is the SECOND
largest suggested source on this video, behind its own series at 29.8%.
Under the old titles every video in the set was the near-identical string
"Rocksmith 2014 | Play Along on Bass | M | ...", so the recommender had no reason
to treat them as distinct destinations to serve in sequence. Unique titles make
them distinguishable and they start clustering.
Consequence: finishing the remaining six is not cosmetic. Each rewrite feeds the
others. Budget it as one job.

Inside the template, in the only paragraph not in square brackets, MAVO had
already written the real value proposition:

   bass so even if you don't have the game you can practice!"
  "I did not play on it to not adding extra bass tone to the sons so easier for
   you to hear out everything well. Kept 5 second on start to show you the tuning
   but also kept on the bottom left during the song..."
That is a genuinely good product - a practice aid for people without the game,
with a tuning reference and the tuner left running. It was sitting in paragraph
four behind a dead download link. The rewrite promotes it to the title. Nothing
about the video had to change, only what it admits to being.

VER 3.9 - 2026-11-01 - CO-0005: ABIGAIL, THE TV TREND, AND THE THUMBNAIL PROBLEM
================================================================================

THE NUMBERS (356 views, 8.8 h, +37 subs, Ineligible / Ads off, GBP 0.00)
  5.4k impressions, CTR 5.2%, 22.2% of impressions from YouTube recommending
  AVD 1:28, APV 47.4%, 63% still watching at 0:30 - above typical
  65.2% YouTube search, 12.6% playlists, 9.8% channel pages, 6.7% suggested
  TV 47.8% / computer 34.9% / mobile 16.1% / tablet 1.2%
  +37 subs from 220 unique viewers = 16.8% conversion

THE CROSS-FEED IS NOW COMPOUNDING
"Content suggesting this video": 75.0% own series (still the machine string),
12.5% the retitled Masterpiece, 4.2% the retitled Another Life.
"Viewers also watch", in order: Another Life (989), Masterpiece (790), then the
untouched siblings at 510 and 333, then a 37-view Short.

**The two rewritten titles have displaced the untouched ones at the top of the
watch-next list on a video nobody has touched.** That is the second independent
measurement of the compounding effect and it is stronger than the first: one
retitle fed a sibling, two retitles now feed a third. At 2 of 8 rewritten, the
two rewritten videos already outrank every untouched one in the watch-next list.

THE TV TREND - THREE CAPTURES, ALL IN ONE DIRECTION
  Masterpiece    TV 34.7% | mobile 34.2% | computer 28.7%
  Another Life   TV 41.5% | mobile 31.0% | computer 26.4%
  Abigail        TV 47.8% | mobile 16.1% | computer 34.9%
TV rises every time, mobile collapses. A play along is a living-room format: the
set goes on, the bass comes out, the phone stays in the pocket. This is the
format working exactly as MAVO described it, and it is now visible three times.
Practical consequence for thumbnails: the viewer is across a room, so the card
has to read at living-room distance. Small text is wasted on this audience.

THE REAL DEFECT IS NOT THE COPY, IT IS THE CARD
CTR is the only metric moving the wrong way: 9.3% -> 8.5% -> 5.2%. Abigail also
has the highest share of recommendation-driven impressions (22.2% vs 10.3%) and
the lowest APV (47.4%), and its end screen CTR is 0.0% against a 1.1% channel
average.
Every thumbnail in the series is near-identical. When the recommender serves the
video to someone less certain, an indistinguishable card cannot rescue the
impression. No amount of description rewriting touches this.
Thumbnails are now the top open item for the format, and end screens are doing
literally nothing on this video - free traffic left on the table.

DELIVERED
  Title  Abigail by Motionless In White, bass part on screen from bar one
         (64 chars, opener "Abigail" used 0 times; third distinct shape in the

VER 4.0 - 2026-11-01 - CO-0006 AMERICA: +58 SUBS FROM 333 VIEWS, AND TWO
                            CORRECTIONS I OWE ON VER 3.9
================================================================================

THE NUMBERS (333 views, 9.0 h, +58 subs, Ineligible / Ads off, GBP 0.00)
  4.3k impressions, CTR 6.1%, 14.5% from YouTube recommending
  AVD 1:37, APV 42.5%, 62% still watching at 0:30 - above typical
  59.8% YouTube search, 17.4% playlists, 11.4% channel pages, 6.0% suggested
  computer 45.0% / TV 33.2% / mobile 16.0% / tablet 5.8%
  +58 subs from 197 unique viewers = 29.4% conversion

THE HEADLINE IS NOT THE VIEWS
29.4% of unique viewers subscribed. That is nearly one in three, and it is the
best conversion measured anywhere in this project:
  Masterpiece    51 / 459 = 11.1%
  Another Life   39 / 534 =  7.3%
  Abigail        37 / 220 = 16.8%
  America        58 / 197 = 29.4%
Conversion RISES as views FALL. Playlist share rises the same way:
8.9 -> 9.8 -> 12.6 -> 17.4%. The small videos get SAVED for practice and
subscribed to; the big ones just get watched.
Running total, four audited play alongs: 2,468 views, +185 subscribers.
That is the answer to MAVO's keep-or-remove question, and it is measured now.

CORRECTION 1 - I OVER-CLAIMED THE TV TREND
Ver 3.9 said TV share was rising 34.7 -> 41.5 -> 47.8 and called it "three
captures, one direction". America reads computer 45.0%, TV 33.2%. The chain
breaks and computer leads. The claim is retracted in CONTEXT rather than quietly
dropped.
What survives: mobile is the smallest real screen in all four captures
(34.2 / 31.0 / 16.1 / 16.0). The honest rule is "a screen people sit in front of,
with mobile a minority throughout" - not "TV is rising".
Open hypothesis: which of TV or desktop leads seems to track the song. Abigail is
TV-led, America is desktop-led, plausibly because drop-D reference material gets
opened next to a DAW or a tab. Test it, do not repeat it as fact.

CORRECTION 2 - CTR IS NOT DECLINING
Ver 3.9 read 9.3 -> 8.5 -> 5.2 as a decline. With America at 6.1% it is plainly
noise in a mid-single-digit band. The thumbnail defect is still real - every card
in the set is near-identical and end screen CTR is 0.0% on both Abigail and
America - but it must be argued from the identical cards and the dead end
screens, not from a falling line that does not exist.
The metric that IS monotonic is APV: 56.2 -> 52.8 -> 47.4 -> 42.5. Retention
share is the genuine open question for the format. The four songs differ in
length and difficulty, so establish the cause before blaming thumbnails or copy.

SEARCH - THE SERIES IS BUILDING ITS OWN QUERY
"rocksmith bass motionless in white" at 1.5% is the largest single search term
recorded anywhere in this project, and it is a COMBINED query - platform plus
artist. That is exactly what the house formula feeds. Supporting it: "motionless
in white america" 0.5% (a real song query) and "rocksmith bass drop d" 0.5%.

A TEMPLATE CAUGHT BEFORE IT SHIPPED
The first title draft was "America by Motionless In White, ..." - which is
ABIGAIL's construction. Two videos in, the set was already forming a shape. It
was rejected at the gate, not in the wild, and the delivered title is a fourth
distinct structure.

VER 4.1 - 2026-11-01 - CO-0007 IMMACULATE MISCONCEPTION: THE APV DIAGNOSIS
                        (and two more retractions)
================================================================================

THE NUMBERS (510 views, 14.5 h, +54 subs, Ineligible / Ads off, GBP 0.00)
  4.9k impressions, CTR 8.0%, 12.7% from YouTube recommending
  AVD 1:43, APV 41.9%, 68% still watching at 0:30 - the HIGHEST opening hold
    of any capture in this series
  64.1% YouTube search, 15.7% playlists, 10.0% channel pages, 4.7% suggested
  TV 33.6% / mobile 32.7% / computer 32.6% - a three-way tie
  +54 subs from 294 unique viewers = 18.4%

THE DIAGNOSIS I HAD BEEN MISSING
APV across the five captures: 56.2 -> 52.8 -> 47.4 -> 42.5 -> 41.9. Falling.
Retention at 0:30 across the same five: 70 -> 63 -> 63 -> 62 -> 68. Stable to
rising.
People are NOT leaving at the start. They are leaving LATER. The average is being
dragged down by the middle and the end of the video.
That kills the two explanations I had been circling:
  - not the thumbnail: a thumbnail cannot affect where in the video people stop
  - not the intro or first frame: that is the healthiest part of the curve
The honest reading: a four-minute play along gets used for a minute or two, the
viewer has what they came for, stops, and comes back later. The rising playlist
share (8.9 -> 17.4%) is the evidence that they come back.
So low APV on a practice aid is a USAGE PATTERN, not a defect. APV is the wrong
primary metric for this format. Watch time per viewer, playlist saves and
subscriber conversion describe what this content actually does.
I should have said this four captures ago instead of building a thumbnail project
on a falling line.

RETRACTION 1 - DEVICES (again)
Ver 4.0 corrected "TV is rising" to "mobile is always the minority". This capture
breaks that too: TV 33.6 / mobile 32.7 / computer 32.6 - a three-way tie inside
one point. The device split is roughly even and unstable between songs.
New rule: no device conclusion may be written from one video, and none may be
repeated until it holds across the whole set. Two retractions in two versions is
enough.

RETRACTION 2 - THE THUMBNAIL PRIORITY
The thumbnail project was justified with a falling CTR and a falling APV. CTR is
noise (9.3 / 8.5 / 5.2 / 6.1 / 8.0) and APV is now explained by usage. Thumbnails
stay a minor tidy-up - end screen CTR is 0.4% against a 1.1% average - but they
are not the priority I called them.

CROSS-FEED - THIRD MEASUREMENT, NOW THE DOMINANT SIGNAL
Suggested traffic from our own rewritten titles:
  CO-0004: one rewritten sibling existed  -> 24.6%
  CO-0005: two existed                    -> 16.7%
  CO-0007: three existed                  -> 37.5% (16.7 Another Life
                                             + 12.5 America + 8.3 Masterpiece)
"Viewers also watch": Another Life, Masterpiece, Abigail, an unrelated Iron
Maiden Short, then America. Four of five visible slots are the four rewritten
videos, in reverse rewrite order. The untouched siblings have dropped off the
list entirely.
Four rewrites in, the rewritten videos ARE the watch-next surface.

SURFACE PROBLEMS FOUND
  - Description carries an extra clickable dead invite this video alone:
    "Until that join our Discord: https://discord.gg/CeDvXXTKJv" - the SAME dead
    invite flagged back in MR-0002. Removed.
  - Tags 21 at 496/500, including a self-brand "mavomusic" tag. Dropped.
  - Pin identical to the other three: three links, weekly schedule, subscribe
    begging, dead discord.gg/Hguy3JCXn.

had to retract two single-metric claims already:
  1. Referrers: xmxbest.pro 6.5%, txxx.com 4.4% (adult tube), desitales2.com
     3.4%, desi-porn.tube 1.6% - known spam/bot domains.
  2. Geography: India 77.6% against UK 2.8-8.7% on the audited clean videos.
  3. Retention: APV 6.7%, AVD 0:16, 10% at 0:30.
  4. CTR 1.6% on 3.0k impressions with 36.2% recommendation-driven.
  5. "Viewers also watch" reads "not enough eligible data" - a panel that works
     on all five clean videos.
I did NOT add the mobile 54.5% device split to that list, because the 4.1 rule
bans device claims from single captures. Five signals carry it without that one.
CONSEQUENCE: the +59 subs are of unknown quality, are not comparable with
America's 29.4%, and the series total stays +239 from five clean videos.

FINDING 2 - THE 0:16 AVERAGE IS THE INTRO, AND IT IS AN EDIT DEFECT
The opening frame is a Rocksmith score/results card: "Replay Score Attack on
Hard", "Raise Your Mastery to 101% in this Song", "Get a 303 Note Streak", a
100% overlay and the MAVOmusic watermark.
Average view duration: exactly 0:16. The song has not started when the average
viewer leaves.
Stated as inference, but 0:16 matching the length of that card is hard to
dismiss. Options for MAVO: trim the opening in Studio's editor, or leave it and
start future play alongs on the song with the score screen moved to the end.
No amount of title, description, tag or pin work can touch this. It is the most
actionable finding in the whole play along audit.

FINDING 3 - THE TAGS CONTRADICT THE DESCRIPTION
The description correctly says: "[DIS] AIMER: This song is an official DLC you
can find inside the game." (typo in the header, but the statement is correct).
The tags say: cdlc, rocksmith 2014 custom, customsong, custom songs, customsfoge,
dic. A licensed DLC track labelled as custom content, using the exact vocabulary
the section 33 CDLC-policy theory is built on.
Also removed: clone hero and guitar hero (competing platforms), relax, sightread,
sight read, charts, bass songs, how to play, and a self-brand mavomusic tag.
Hypothesis, deliberately not called a finding: the Guitar Hero / Clone Hero tags
may be part of why 36.2% of impressions came from recommending this to an
audience that did not want it. Only testable by removing them and re-reading.

FINDING 4 - THE PREMIERE GAVE IT NOTHING
Peak 3 concurrent, average 3. A Premiere puts a video's first impression inside a
live format; this one debuted to an empty room.

FINDING 5 - BOTH SEARCH TERMS ARE WRONG-INTENT
"green day live" 8.3% is a CONCERT query. "green day rocksmith" 8.3% is the right
one. Half its search traffic carried the wrong intent.

THE APV RULE NEEDS A CONDITION
Ver 4.1 said low APV is a usage pattern. True for the five clean 2022 videos
(0:30 hold 62-70%). False here (6.7% APV, 10% at 0:30 - broken curve).
Refined, not retracted: APV is a usage pattern ONLY when the 0:30 hold is healthy.
If the opening hold has collapsed, APV is a real defect and it belongs to the edit.
Always read 0:30 first, then APV.

DISCORD - MAVO RECREATED THE INVITE
MAVO supplied https://discord.gg/ESGsGkN4j2. Verified: that is already the handle
in 568 places across the live corpus, and NO dead invite (Hguy3JCXn, CeDvXXTKJv,
sHtflKrX, CebyXXTKJv) appears in a single one of the 308 live packages. The dead
codes survive only inside the log files as evidence, which is where they belong.
The LIVE PINS on the videos still carry Hguy3JCXn though - those get replaced
when the new pins are pasted.

DELIVERED
  Title  Green Day's 'X-Kid' and the bass Mike Dirnt wrote for it
         (56 chars, opener "Green" 0 prior uses, sixth distinct shape; names
         Dirnt, safe - founding bassist of 38 years, uncontroversial)
  Desc   1,195 chars; emoji stripped, [KICK]/[TWITCH]/[SCHEDULE] and the full
         weekly EST schedule deleted, the typo'd [DISC] AIMER block rewritten
         into the real value statement: official DLC means the part on screen is
         the real transcription, not a community approximation
  Tags   27 at 498/500 -> 16 at 249, restoring 251 characters of headroom on a
         box that was completely full
  Pin    dead invite and schedule gone, zero URLs

MR-0009 logged, E4 roll-up 19 -> 20.
Series 6 of 8. Remaining: Black Parade (504), .38 Special (1,347).

HONEST PRIORITY: of all eight, X-Kid is the LOWEST priority for the copy above,
because the audience it reached was largely not real and it stays ineligible
regardless. It is the HIGHEST priority for the two things copy cannot fix - the
intro edit and the CDLC tags. Black Parade and .38 Special deserve the effort.

================================================================================
VER 4.3 - 2026-11-01 - CO-0009 BLACK PARADE: THE PSARC OFFER, MAVO'S PAID-TRAFFIC
                        CORRECTION, AND A CONFIRMED 2024 PRODUCTION HABIT
================================================================================

THE MOST URGENT THING IN THIS WHOLE AUDIT IS ONE SENTENCE OF COPY
The live description contains, under a typo'd [DISC]LAIMER heading:

  "This song was an official DLC you could buy from Ubisoft. I made my own version
   but cannot be sharable on Ignition so hit me up with a message on Discord for
   the psarc less than three"

A psarc is the Rocksmith package file. In plain terms: this is licensed Ubisoft
content, I converted it, message me and I will send it to you.
That is a public offer to redistribute a licensed file, on a monetised video, on a
channel already fighting Content ID claims. Of everything found in this corpus it
is the strongest candidate for a POLICY flag rather than a claim, and unlike a
claim it takes thirty seconds to fix.
ACTION: delete the paragraph today. Do not reword it. Check the other 2024
uploads for the same offer before anything else is pasted.

MAVO'S CORRECTION - AND MY RETRACTION
MAVO explained the strange referrers: "Done back in the day Fiverr campaigns so
that might tell about weird sites and fake engagements. I ordered boost but had no
idea where it coming from. Later on same with YouTube Adsense Ads from Studio. I
paid for youtube to advertise my videos, the source unknown mostly."

So the X-Kid finding was WRONG IN ATTRIBUTION. I described it as "not a real
audience" arriving from spam and adult-tube domains, as though something had been
done to the channel. It is paid promotion MAVO bought, from suppliers whose supply
he did not control. Nobody attacked anything and it should never be described that
way.

The CLASSIFICATION survives, because the analysis purpose has not changed: paid
traffic is not organic demand, so it still cannot sit in organic averages or be
used to judge whether a format works. The register now calls it PAID-PROMOTION
INFLUENCE, with the correction logged above the row rather than quietly patched.

WHAT THE PAID TRAFFIC ACTUALLY BOUGHT
  3.0k impressions, CTR 1.6%, APV 6.7% on X-Kid.
  4.1k impressions, CTR 2.7%, APV 8.2% on Black Parade.
Impressions arrived. Watch time, ranking and revenue did not. Both are ineligible,
both earn nothing, and both carry the worst retention in the set.
Hypothesis, stated as one: a large block of viewers leaving inside thirty seconds
is a negative retention signal to the recommender, so cheap promotion may be
teaching YouTube that these videos do not hold people. We cannot see inside the
system, but the direction is consistent across both videos.

BLACK PARADE IS NOT X-KID, AND THAT MATTERS FOR PRIORITISING
Black Parade has a real audience under the paid noise:
  - Correct search terms: "welcome to the black parade bass" 5.3% and
    "rocksmith bass" 5.3%. X-Kid's best term was "green day live" - a concert query.
  - "Viewers also watch" resolves, and all five entries are our own videos. X-Kid
    cannot produce that report at all.
  - Browse 12.1% and channel pages 11.3% are real YouTube surfaces.
So: paid promotion on top of a small audience, rather than paid promotion instead
of one. Worth fixing. X-Kid is not.

THE OPENING DEFECT IS A HABIT, NOT A MISHAP
Both 2024 uploads open on a score/results card with a 100% overlay before the song
starts:
  X-Kid:        AVD 0:16, APV 6.7%, 10% at 0:30
  Black Parade: AVD 0:27, APV 8.2%, 15% at 0:30
  The five 2022 videos, which open on the song: 62-70% at 0:30.
Rule now written: every play along starts on the first note. Cards go at the end
or not at all.

DELIVERED
  Title  How Mikey Way holds 'Welcome To The Black Parade' together on bass
         (66 chars, opener "How" used 0 times in 308 packages, seventh distinct
         shape, and it carries the exact phrase the analytics prove people search)
  Desc   1,217 chars; psarc offer deleted outright; typo'd disk-laimer replaced
         with the safe true statement that this is official licensed DLC
  Tags   28 at 500/500 -> 15 at 280, restoring 220 characters. Same
         self-contradiction as X-Kid removed (official DLC described, then tagged
         cdlc / custom / customsforge / guitar hero / clone hero)
  Pin    dead invite and weekly schedule gone, zero URLs

MR-0010 logged, E4 roll-up 20 -> 21.
Series 7 of 8. Remaining: .38 Special - If I'd Been The One (1,347 views,
11-04-2024), the highest-traffic play along in the set.

FOLDER RESTRUCTURE - _All Songs NOW BUCKETED IN TENS
MAVO asked for it, and it is done:
  _All Songs/

    0011 - 0020/                    empty, .gitkeep
    0021 - 0030/                    empty, .gitkeep
All 118 moved paths are tracked by git as RENAMES, so file history is preserved.
Empty ranges need .gitkeep or git will not store them.
Past 0100, one more level: _All Songs/0001 - 0100/0001 - 0010/...
A README.md now documents the convention, the internal per-song layout, and the
two-numbering-system warning.
Nothing reads these paths programmatically - PY/ only touches bands/, TXT/ and the
kit - so buckets can be renamed or re-nested without breaking a build.

================================================================================
VER 4.5 - 2026-11-01 - CO-0011 CHOPIN: THE ONLY VIDEO THAT EARNS, AND WHY IT
                        STILL ONLY EARNS 54 PENCE
================================================================================

A CORRECTION I OWE FIRST
My register entry called this a ONE-HOUR loop. It is a FOUR-HOUR loop. MAVO:
"it is 4 hours not 1 hour loop. I can do more in the future if works out."
The runtime (4:00:33) and the video's own title both said four hours. The length
is not a detail here - it IS the product - so the entry is corrected in place
rather than quietly patched over.

THE NUMBERS (4,887 views, 1,900 watch hours, +64 subs, GBP 0.54, E2 revenue share)
  7.7k impressions, CTR 2.6%, 10.7% from YouTube recommending
  AVD 23:46, APV 9.9%, 101% at 0:30 (above typical - that is looping behaviour)
  SUGGESTED 60.9%, END SCREENS 31.0%, channel pages 5.6%, playlists 0.6%
  YouTube search: 0%
  mobile 91.1%, computer 5.2%, tablet 3.7%, TV 0%
  India 33.1%, Indonesia 3.5%, Pakistan 2.6%, Bangladesh 2.6%, Brazil 2.1%
  CPM GBP 3.97, RPM GBP 0.11
  skippable ads 72.5%, non-skippable 14.5%, bumper 8.9%, display 2.8%

THE FINDING THAT REFRAMES THE WHOLE VIDEO
60.9% of traffic is suggested, and the five visible sources are:
  "E Tem Mais | Derrota nas urnas..."                      (Brazilian news)
  "Imran Khan Attacked in Pakistan: Ex-Pak PM Sho..."
  "Imran Khan's first visuals after shooting attack..."
  "Gravitas: The Gunman who attacked Imran Khan"
  "Imran Khan Shot At: Imran Khan Injured In Firing..."
FOUR OF THE FIVE are breaking-news videos about the Imran Khan shooting of
03-11-2022 - the day after this loop was published.

The loop was served next to rolling news and that is where its audience came from.
That one fact explains everything else: India/Pakistan/Bangladesh/Indonesia
geography, mobile 91.1%, passive 23-minute average view duration, 101% at 0:30
from seeking and looping, and an RPM of 11 pence driven by geography.

WHY IT EARNS, AND WHY IT EARNS LITTLE
IT EARNS because the composition is public domain. Chopin died in 1849, nothing
claims the audio bed, Content ID has nothing to match. That part stands exactly.
IT EARNS LITTLE because the audience it landed is cheap:
  RPM 0.11 means about GBP 10 per 90,000 views.
  Four hours of looping produced 54 pence.
So the rule is now TWO rules, not one:
  1. RIGHTS decide ELIGIBILITY. Unclaimed repertoire is the only lane that pays.
  2. AUDIENCE decides REVENUE. A loop served to news traffic earns geographic
     CPM, not content value.
Worth writing down because I spent two versions implying the first one was the
whole story.

MAVO'S QUESTION - "I can do more in the future if works out"
  1. DO IT. Public-domain repertoire is the only genuinely eligible lane here -
     22 of the 23 long videos audited are ineligible. That is not a small thing.
  2. IT ALREADY BOUGHT 1,900 WATCH HOURS, the largest watch-time asset measured
     in this project. Real value whatever the 54 pence.
  3. IT DOES NOT BUY MONEY AT SCALE on this audience. 11p RPM is geography.
  4. CHANGE ONE THING NEXT TIME: aim at an audience that SEARCHES. This video has
     0% YouTube search traffic and depends on one week of breaking news from
     November 2022. A loop titled and tagged for "classical music for studying"
     and "chopin nocturne" earns from search, which keeps arriving after the news
     cycle dies. That is the difference between a lucky upload and a series.
  5. HONEST CEILING: at this RPM, ten more loops might produce a few pounds.
     Treat the lane as an eligibility and watch-time play, not an income stream.
THE STRATEGIC ASYMMETRY, said plainly: this loop earns today but is 0% search
driven and news-dependent. The play alongs cannot earn but are 60-70% search
driven and keep arriving. Long term the ineligible series is the more durable
asset.

CDLC THEORY NARROWED A THIRD TIME
This description names CDLC and CustomsForge explicitly, and tags carry cdlc and
customsforge - and it monetises as E2 revenue share.
So DESCRIBING where a chart came from does not block a video. What Black Parade
does is different and worse: it OFFERS TO SEND THE FILE.
RULE: a CDLC reference is not fatal; a redistribution offer is urgent. Stop
writing this as a vocabulary problem.

THIRD DEAD INVITE FOUND
The description carries discord.gg/afnuBTBxiq, which nobody had flagged. That is
three dead invites in play: Hguy3JCXn (pins), afnuBTBxiq (this description),
CebyXXTKJv/sHtflKrX (older files).

DELIVERED
  Title  Four hours of Chopin's Nocturne Op. 9 No. 2, played on bass
         (59 chars, opener "Four" 0 prior uses, ninth distinct shape). Keeps the
         length in the title because the length is the product.
  Desc   1,533 chars. The four musicology paragraphs are KEPT - they are the only
         real substance in any description in this corpus and they earn trust.
         Everything around them rebuilt: the loop is sold as a loop, provenance
         stated honestly without naming a distribution site, all three stale
         invites gone.
  Tags   27 at 493/500 -> 15 at 233. Biggest tag change in the audit. Leads with
         classical and study queries the video has never had; every CDLC and
         competitor-platform tag removed.
  Pin    108 chars, zero URLs, asks the question that produces the next video.
